# Content-based sandbox inventory

Inspected 28553 files: {".md": 484, ".py": 1457, ".json": 10965, ".csv": 7135, ".npz": 8484, ".xlsx": 23, ".yml": 3, ".yaml": 2}. Content-positive matches: 6243. Inspection errors: 0. Runtime environments, Git internals, build caches and this clean publication checkout were excluded. The working project and historical archive trees were included.

Python/Markdown/configuration text was read; JSON nested keys/values parsed; CSV headers and up to5 data rows checked; every workbook sheet header and first2 rows inspected; every NPZ member's NPY shape/dtype inspected without unpickling or recomputing arrays. No matching pickle/joblib files occurred. These are existence/schema checks, not a new patient analysis.

The table includes each content-positive file and each selected source. Potentially identity-bearing filenames/paths are replaced by local-only file references. The full path mapping and full 28553-file inventory stay local and are not uploaded. This public inventory contains file metadata only, no patient values. For data artifacts the outcome column means an outcome field was found, not that a model was fitted. Generic field references in code do not themselves constitute patient data. Archived files are published as sanitized source copies; originals and all raw outputs stay local.

| --- | --- |
| .md | 484 |
| .py | 1457 |
| .json | 10965 |
| .csv | 7135 |
| .npz | 8484 |
| .xlsx | 23 |
| .yml | 3 |
| .yaml | 2 |
